Working on CTR2WUP¶

Purpose and starting context¶

The product is a Windows injector GUI that converts one supported, owned 3DS revision into a playable per-game Wii U WUP. Original diagnostics and synthetic fixtures prove components; they are not commercial compatibility or MVP acceptance. Do not implement the GUI before the execution/conversion pipeline is demonstrated.

At the start of a session:

  1. Read STATUS.md for the latest verified checkpoint and pending evidence.
  2. Read docs/development-plan.md, the relevant docs/milestones.md gate and docs/development-tracking.md. The user requirements are in docs/project-brief.md; docs/gui-workflow.md defines the intended product UI.
  3. Inspect git status --short --branch, current PRs and Project #2. Preserve existing user edits. Use the existing isolated checkout; no worktrees unless asked.
  4. Run make help and python3 -m host doctor when tool availability is uncertain. In a managed cloud environment, read its runtime skill and inspect current network/credential availability before diagnosing access failures.

Use docs/repository-guide.md for the source map and reader routes. Prefer the specific runtime contract over old development history when implementing behavior.

Scope and evidence¶

  • Choose a concrete feature under an existing task. Give it a native parent, repository milestone and Project membership, explicit acceptance criteria and an associated PR. Keep docs/features.json current; use scoped Closes #... references and Refs #... for unfinished broad tasks. Do not close a milestone because one feature passed. Project fields own live status.
  • Continue independent work when physical testing is deferred. Record the exact pending console/input/Windows evidence instead of making it a blanket blocker.
  • Keep shared CPU/services/renderer contracts reusable. Avoid title-specific patches or silent successful stubs. Unsupported CPU/services/GPU state must fail visibly and preserve the documented atomicity boundaries.
  • Never claim console execution from a cross-build or host pass. Structural package checks do not establish authenticity or installer acceptance.
  • Synthetic programs, scenes and fixtures must be original. Respect dependency pins, licenses and notices; preserve signature/checksum/TLS verification.
  • Keep ROMs, keys, assets, translated commercial code, saves, packages, traces and credentials in ignored private directories. Never put them in source, public docs, logs or artifacts. Use artifacts/ for generated original evidence.

Build and validation¶

Run focused checks during implementation, then required make check (Python and all normal/ASan/UBSan C suites) and make prototype (RPX/WUHB) before merge. Automatic Required CI and Documentation build must also pass.

Toolchain setup is bash tools/install-wiiu-sdk.sh; the optional pinned host lifter is bash tools/install-translator.sh. Host fixtures do not require the Wii U SDK. Original compiled-homebrew/reference validators document their own dependency pins and use fresh output directories; retain their reports and frames. If the execution sandbox prevents LeakSanitizer under ptrace, use the supported execution escalation. Do not disable sanitizer checks to report a pass.

Update affected contracts, compatibility limits, STATUS.md, CHANGELOG.md, versioned metadata and milestone evidence. Keep the current plan concise; put chronological background in docs/development-history.md. Validate maintained docs with python3 tools/build-docs-site.py using docs-requirements.txt.

Visible delivery and GitHub access¶

  • Develop on a focused topic branch. Commit useful checkpoints and push a draft PR early; do not leave substantial development visible only in a local checkout.
  • For Codex commits, install the opt-in hook with python3 tools/codex-attribution.py install, then use git codex-commit. Preserve existing hooks and human attribution; do not rewrite old history solely for attribution. See CONTRIBUTING.md.
  • Use the Actions management credential through immutable trusted-main workflows for GitHub management. Keep its value in Actions. The connected integration supplies source transport, PR lifecycle and workflow dispatch/read. Never run arbitrary PR code with the management secret or copy it locally.
  • Run the Development traceability workflow dry-run first, inspect its receipt, apply the reviewed catalog, then verify native parent/PR relationships, milestone assignment and Project membership. Preserve conflicting owner data.
  • After every merge, delete the completed remote and local topic branch and verify both are absent. If a publication rejects a concurrent write, dispatch a fresh trusted-main run; do not force-push over another publication.
  • Finish with the delivered behavior, linked issues/PRs, validation evidence and material remaining gates. Never substitute test counts for product acceptance.

Maintained source: AGENTS.md · 0b5e01cd0abc. GitHub source and issue links may require repository access.

Security policy¶

CTR2WUP processes local binary containers, private key material and generated artifacts. Reports involving out-of-bounds parsing, private data exposure, arbitrary host-path access or unsafe build/packaging behavior are security issues.

Report privately¶

Use Security → Report a vulnerability on this repository if GitHub private vulnerability reporting is enabled. If that option is unavailable, ask the maintainer for a private reporting route in a normal issue without exploit details, affected private data or attachments. Do not assume an issue is private because repository access is currently restricted.

Include the affected commit/version, platform, impact and a minimal reproduction using original synthetic input. Do not submit ROMs, personal keys, key databases, commercial assets, saves, translated game code or credentials. Describe required private data by type instead of attaching it. If credentials or keys were exposed, remove shared copies and rotate credentials where possible before discussing the incident publicly.

Support boundaries¶

The current development branch is the maintenance target. No production security support window or stable release series has been established. Prefer the latest development build when reproducing a report, and state when the problem also affects an older version. A reviewed fix and tests are needed before a report can be considered resolved.

Container hashes and package structure checks establish integrity within their documented scope; they do not establish signature authenticity, safe installation or compatibility with a commercial game. Preserve the private-input boundaries in AGENTS.md and the limitations in architecture.

Maintained source: SECURITY.md · 0b5e01cd0abc. GitHub source and issue links may require repository access.

Changelog¶

All releases are development increments. RPX/WUHB diagnostics and desktop passes do not establish commercial-game compatibility or installable WUP acceptance.

Evidence follow-up — 2026-10-13¶

  • Review physical Audio Check/GX Transfers 0.20.0 and Guest Draws 0.23.0 passes, separate picture/Menu-return confirmation, and silence/native-tone results.
  • Record the 57.33-second drawn presentation and premature visual-prompt guard; plan bounded guest progress and separate execution/scaling/wait/storage metrics.
  • Replace completed test handoffs with current evidence and remaining gates.
  • Document the Windows/Python small-intake route for the oversized reportedly decrypted CCI. No uploaded ROM byte, key or private trace is published.

0.23.0 — 2026-10-13¶

  • Execute original compiled guest PICA draws through standard GSP with tracked physical resources, canonical tiled RGBA8 targets and retained shader/state.
  • Commit pixels/state/counters/completion atomically; reject unsupported state, permission/ownership violations and resource/command/relay feedback aliases.
  • Check every original guest target and displayed pixel; retain independent Python image comparisons, ASan/UBSan execution, native builds and exact source provenance in CI's compiled Guest Draws artifact and focused console guide.
  • Preserve raw VFP push/pop bits under CRT FPSCR controls while continuing to reject unsupported arithmetic modes.
  • Connect scoped feature issues, native parents, milestones, Project membership and historical/active PRs through reviewed trusted-main Actions with audit receipts.
  • Refine AGENTS.md, repository tour, contributor policy and current plan; centralize portable guest runtime sources across host, test, validator and native builds.
  • Keep physical rendering/audio retests, general PICA/GX2 translation, commercial conversion and all full milestone acceptance open.

0.22.0 — 2026-10-13¶

  • Execute first original PICA-format loader/index/fixed/uniform/MOV shader scenes through an owned software triangle boundary, with atomic state/pixel commit.
  • Restrict numeric and raster behavior to exact supported values; reject unknown pipeline state, undefined inputs and unsupported shader/fragment operations.
  • Compare both index formats against exact independent pixels and 12,290 pinned nihstro encodings. Add normal/sanitizer/Windows scene coverage and image receipts.
  • Build a focused native Scene Check with automatic pixel checks, console timings, two-screen picture prompts and retained TXT results. Publish its CI artifact.
  • Keep guest GSP draws, GPU shader translation and full M4 acceptance open.

0.21.0 — 2026-10-13¶

  • Gather bounded vertex streams through unsigned 8-bit/16-bit little-endian indices, retaining duplicates/order and validating all referenced bytes before output changes. Protect complete index spans and descriptors against aliases.
  • Exercise every index value plus original reordered multi-stream/interleaved fixtures, unreferenced nonfinite data, late failure, limits and arithmetic.
  • Keep PICA loader registers, fixed attributes, shaders, primitive assembly and draw submission visibly unsupported; full renderer acceptance remains open.
  • Record Actions-based GitHub management and completed-branch deletion policy.

0.20.0 — 2026-10-13¶

  • Initialize and release a neutral native AX context in every diagnostic; compiled guests acquire no tone voice. Record lifecycle/failure counters and add a two-step Audio Check for launch silence and the native tone.
  • Record reviewed physical TXT collection, original fresh-launch save reload, embedded/SD assets and GX transfer/configuration. Preserve older failures; launch-music regression and full platform acceptance remain open.
  • Clarify the separate fresh-launch save step and simplify the next test handoff.
  • Populate Project #2 using its Actions secret, preserve owner values and add a live stacked task chart with visible timestamps and interactive dashboard.
  • Prepare verified public docs-only Pages publication through trusted main, while application source stays private. Rewrite the README around purpose, capabilities, getting started, progress and contributions.

0.19.0 — 2026-10-12¶

  • Add an original bounded vertex-stream decoder for the four published PICA component formats, explicit input permutation, defaults and transactional finite-float/range/alias validation. Loader registers, shaders and draws remain unsupported.
  • Make the selected existing GitHub Project the status/acceptance authority; source synchronization now updates scope/evidence only. Add preserved Project overview, priority/work-type fields, filtered views and a secure aggregate dashboard/export/publication workflow. Live setup requires working credentials.
  • Replace the README's static progress image with the authoritative Project entry point; keep the implementation estimate separate from task/gate counts.
  • Deliver a focused, hash-verified console handoff combining current GX/PICA diagnostics with the retained Save/result/RomFS fixes and optional SD assets. Individual app versions are explicit; no new physical success is inferred.

0.18.0 — 2026-10-12¶

  • Add original bounded native PICA inspection and transactional finalized raw viewport/scissor state, with standard GX command-list/P3D queue integration.
  • Reject unsupported draws/uploads/flags before state changes; record parser diagnostics in the existing guided TXT report.

0.17.0 — 2026-10-12¶

CTR2WUP status¶

Updated October 12, 2026 (America/New_York); report filenames use UTC. Requirements: docs/project-brief.md; product plan: docs/development-plan.md; GUI workflow: docs/gui-workflow.md. The user resumed implementation and selected Super Mario 3D Land as the first title candidate. The target remains an UWUVCI-style Windows GUI: select a game (decrypted or locally prepared with imported 3DS material), configure title/artwork, click Inject, install its per-game WUP and launch from Wii U Menu. The CLI is the shared engine, not the final product.

Overall implementation estimate: approximately 20%, reassessed against the v0.16.0 GUI-to-playable-WUP target on October 12, 2026. This is a rough engineering judgment, not measured task completion. 0/7 full milestones are accepted. See the completion assessment for its basis, remaining work, and external testing gates; the README provides a concise progress overview.

Version 0.16.0 advances asset preparation and texture decoding while the user is away. These are shared foundations for the GUI's future injection/runtime pipeline. No commercial profile, PICA draw renderer, Mario build or installed WUP is enabled. The earlier verbal console successes remain usable reported evidence; no new physical run is claimed. No ROM or key upload is requested now.

  • Full RomFS preparation: canonical NCCH IVFC header/master anchoring and every hash/data block are verified, including final padding. Bounded Level-3 metadata validates Unicode names, hash/parent/sibling chains, duplicates, cycles/orphans and file extents. prepare-rom now applies this when RomFS is present; prepare-assets exports a private romfs.bin and manifest containing source/code identity and file/blob hashes. Publication refuses replacement and preserves the source. Virtual filenames never address host extraction paths. Integrity is distinct from signature authenticity; unsupported layouts fail.
  • File-backed runtime assets: original portable SHA-256 and immutable file provider require the configured blob hash before mounting into existing read-only guest FS transport. Guest paths remain virtual. Live views/handles block close; teardown closes guest ownership before the file. The standard-C provider is limited to LONG_MAX (2 GiB minus one on 32-bit Wii U) and checks integrity at open, assuming immutable backing. Console I/O cost is unmeasured.
  • Texture foundation: original C/Python decoding of all 14 base formats, 8×8 Morton tiles, packed channels, luminance/alpha, RG8/HILO, ETC1/ETC1A4. Base dimensions are powers of two in 8..1024. Byte order is explicit; buffers, aliases and invalid compressed differential blocks are preflighted before output changes. Sampling/mips/cache/GX2 upload/draws remain unfinished.

Validation is preserved under artifacts/reports/assets-textures-v016/:

  • Required make check: 206 Python tests, including 16 new RomFS and six texture cases, plus sixteen C suites normally and with ASan/UBSan. New provider/SHA suite: 3,467 assertions; texture suite: 7,959 checks.
  • New C suites pass Clang normal/sanitizer and emulated big-endian PPC checks. The texture matrix compares 42 original images byte-for-byte with Python; 39 also match pinned external Zakuro. HILO is explicitly excluded because CTR2WUP follows Azahar's RG8 convention. The emulated PPC matrix has identical source/output hashes to all 42 host vectors. No external emulator code is linked or included in runtime/delivery.

Three intentionally unavailable startup IPC requests remain recorded; a PASS does not claim all services are implemented.

A separate fixed-size result-<app>-0.14.6-<session>-<suffix>.txt now bypasses the full trace handler. Exclusive launch identity, checked write/flush/close and exact 4 KiB read-back precede RESULT VERIFIED. Missing/replaced files fail visibly without recreation; first errors pause guest execution. Handler registration is checked. Guest statistics are captured before cleanup, and result writes finish before system transition. Existing trace sync/close/volume commits and WUT mount ownership remain. Read-back is not a physical power-loss durability guarantee. The exact cause of the missing v0.14.5 logs is unknown.

Save publication no longer depends on rename replacing a destination: it stages exclusively, validates/moves the old primary to .previous, publishes into the vacant primary, and removes the backup after success. Failed publication attempts rollback; missing-primary load can recover validated .previous. Corrupt primary, stale .pending and ambiguous backup fail visibly and remain preserved. Tests model no-overwrite rename, repeat updates, rollback failure and abrupt interruption. This corrects an identified WUT/FSA assumption; it is not a confirmed diagnosis of the user's exact console save failure. The guest names its failing phase. See docs/filesystem.md.

New current-process read-only Level-3 RomFS transport advances M3 without a ROM/game patch. An original ordinary devkitARM/libctru/newlib application reads root/nested/Unicode assets, seeks/checks EOF, lists directories and rejects writes. Library parsing runs unchanged in guest code. Provider lifetimes, copied file handles, whole-buffer bounds/permissions, IPC aliases, >4 GiB length/offsets and failed-read atomicity are checked. Signature authentication, commercial asset integration, encrypted cartridge access and commercial asset integration remain unfinished.

That actual program required a synthetic read-only shared time page, defined lowest-base ARM STM writeback and ARMv6 U=1/A=0 unaligned single-data access. These are shared implementations with strict alignment retained for fetch/multiple/ exclusive/FP operations, not library patches. The time epoch is explicitly synthetic; no actual RTC/timezone accuracy is claimed. See docs/romfs-assets.md, docs/cpu-stack.md and docs/guest-memory.md.

Verified implementation¶

  • Normal devkitARM/libctru/newlib boot reaches main and exits after 12,416 instructions/35 SVCs. Real libctru threads/LightLock/initialized C TLS pass in 19,989 instructions/40 SVCs. Standard input passes 40,539 instructions/38 SVCs. Graphics and input-driven interactive guests produce all expected pixels in 3,653,198/3,653,794 instructions, with zero fallback; unavailable APT/FS lookups remain explicit. The interactive application also matches on emulated PPC Linux; real compiled threads pass Clang. The scoped native physical results and remaining compiled/save/exit gates are recorded above.

  • Eight process-local thread slots have separate CPU/TLS/raw FP state and waits, strict priority/32-instruction quanta, retained handles/joins, stack pins, recursive mutex ownership and exit cleanup. Address arbiters/local quota output preflight are checked. The thread API has 310 normal/sanitized assertions; the original three-thread probe has 2,694 external boundary assertions, and FP contexts have a separate 61-assertion external arithmetic probe. All native diagnostics embed GUEST THREAD SCHEDULER/thread_selftest.

  • User-mode ARM LDM/STM, Thumb16 PUSH/POP/LDMIA/STMIA and original-AOT register BLX calls use checked word access and interworking. Base writeback occurs after successful transfers; partial faults preserve earlier words and the original base. Empty/user-bank/unpredictable forms fail visibly; v0.13 implements ARM STM's PC+8 source value. 160 API assertions plus the nested-call self-test pass; the external comparison has 352 normal/fault assertions and 48 invalid-Thumb assertions. The independent reference has 220 instruction cases and a 47-instruction full-program trace. Thumb long-immediate calls, complete ARM/VFP and actual PowerPC timings remain open.

  • GSP relay registration returns a retained read/write shared page and client0. Framebuffer updates commit both screens atomically at the diagnostic VBlank; IRQ2/3 wrap/full/coalesced cases, exclusive invalidation, source faults and typed resource/lifetime rollback are checked. All Wii U builds embed the relay self-test and queued original-AOT display guest. 762 API assertions and the original self-test pass normally and with sanitizers. The external comparison has 11,541 assertions, 1,383,167 instructions and 34 SVCs per path, zero fallback and four reference images; GCC/sanitizers, Clang and emulated PPC pass. No complete stock service startup, real interrupt timing, physical execution, GX commands or PICA is claimed.

  • Standard direct GSP acquire/release/force-black/SetBufferSwap checks rights, modes and readable source extents before state changes. Presentation handles rotated RGBA8/BGR8/RGB565/RGB5A1/RGBA4, padding and adjacent mappings; invalid source leaves host output unchanged. 508 API assertions (including v0.13 checked coherent CPU cache flush/invalidation) and the original HID/GSP graphics self-test pass normally and under sanitizers. The external display comparison has 3,603 assertions including four reference PPMs, and passes Clang/emulated PPC. All Wii U builds embed the self-test and original AOT display guest. GX commands, physical/VRAM translation, PICA and stock GPU rendering remain absent. Scoped compiled CPU-framebuffer startup is now verified separately. No console run or gameplay is claimed.

  • Checked unsigned immediate preindexed ARM LDRH/STRH without writeback works in interpreter/original AOT and is exercised by the external graphics probe. Conditions, golden halfwords, alignment/permissions, fault PCs and unsupported forms are checked; this does not complete ARM transfer semantics.

  • HID GetHandles returns checked shared/event copies; shared input is guest read-only. The original ARM consumer maps, waits, reads pad/circle/touch and unmaps/closes/exits through standard interfaces. 858 external boundary and 336 API assertions cover state/bytes/lifetimes, transitions, wrap timestamps, invalid input and capacity rollback. GCC/sanitizers, Clang and emulated PPC checks pass. All Wii U builds embed its self-test and a live original-AOT VPAD-to-ARM consumer. Physical input, timing and shutdown remain unverified; motion/New-family controls remain absent; compiled libctru HID input is now verified separately.

  • Shared/mutex/semaphore handles share the generation-tagged table. Mappings and waits retain objects independently of handles. Heap-backed shared objects pin their source allocation; aliases enforce rights and report Shared state. Shared backing shares the 64 MiB budget. Wait-any selects the first ready object; wait-all consumes nothing until every distinct object is ready. Close/stale/invalid/timeout/resource failures are checked. This does not implement cross-process ownership or priority inheritance. v0.13 adds the separate bounded guest-thread scheduler and owner-aware locks.

  • The original 148-instruction ARM shared/sync probe matches state/memory across interpreter/original/external AOT with 639 boundary assertions and zero fallback. The 197 kernel API assertions and probe pass GCC, sanitizers, Clang and emulated PPC Linux. All three RPX/WUHB builds embed its original SHARED/SYNC LIFECYCLE and kernel_selftest result. Console/native Windows acceptance remains pending.

  • ControlMemory/QueryMemory enforce page bounds, ownership and permissions, with zero-filled first-fit heap/Old-family linear CPU backing. Partial free/ protection preflight mappings and capacity atomically. Loader image/stack/TLS cannot be freed or reprotected. Guard/write-only permissions, holes, resource limits and retained-backing release are tested. Limits are 16 allocations, 32 fragments and 64 MiB total retained backing. Linear GPU/DSP physical-address translation remains absent; local shared aliases are now implemented.

  • The original heap probe executes 151 instructions, 14 memory SVCs and exit. External/original AOT and interpreter CPU/mapping/backing match at 1/7/23 budgets; 1,778 assertions cover lifecycle and protected/freed guest-store fault PCs with zero fallback. Ordinary data mapping changes preserve AOT snapshots; executable mapping/protection and executable-alias writes still invalidate. The current 306 heap API assertions pass GCC, sanitizers, Clang and emulated PPC Linux. All three RPX/WUHB builds embed GUEST HEAP LIFECYCLE/heap_selftest results; physical Wii U and native Windows acceptance remain pending.

  • ARM user read-only CP15 TLS access works in interpreter/original/external AOT. The external ABI refreshes TLS on execution entry and rejects unsupported cache operations even when the pinned emitter would erase them. v0.13 explicitly implements user ARMv6 DMB/DSB ordering for checked CPU backing.

  • Typed, reference-counted service sessions share the generation-tagged handle table. Checked ConnectToPort/SendSyncRequest supports SRV registration/CFG lookup and configured region/model/language reads. It rejects malformed messages, unsupported commands, wrong handles and invalid/aliased output. The Old2DS model ID is verified against libctru (3; New3DS2 is rejected).

  • The original 89-instruction ARM startup probe executes five IPC requests/nine SVCs with matching state/memory through all three backends. 297 external boundary assertions include TLS changed after attach; 35 CP15 assertions cover conditional skips and explicit cache/DMB/DSB/ISB failures. These and the 1,001 service API assertions pass on Clang and emulated big-endian PPC Linux/QEMU. All three RPX/WUHB builds embed the original interpreter/AOT service self-test and native-mode/SD-log result. Physical service execution remains unverified.

  • Original bounded CIA parsing verifies metadata tables, ticket/TMD identity, sparse bitmap/content extents and plaintext hashes; selects executable index 0 and rejects base-title/encryption failures explicitly. Checked loader offsets and external lifter analysis/C generation work for original CIA fixtures.

  • prepare-intake creates a small derived executable CXI/receipt ZIP, omitting RomFS/tickets/certificates/keys and preserving decompressed executable revision. Encryption produces a report-only archive. Source and prior outputs are preserved.

  • The separate .NET console helper produces identical derived CXI bytes for tested inputs, including compressed code and >32 MiB original placeholder data. It passes 13 Linux .NET cases; the self-contained Windows x64 EXE is cross-published with its .NET 10.0.12 runtime/notices. It needs no separate Python/SDK/runtime installation. Native Windows execution remains unverified; it is an intake utility, not the final GUI/injector.

  • The clean pinned fearkov/3dsrecomp checkout is called through an original Rust generation-only driver. A build receipt checks source/driver/binary hashes. Generated C is adapted to an original ABI 4 implementation; the upstream header and tool binary are not bundled. Missing upstream redistribution notices remain an external-tool bundling issue.

  • The checked C engine uses fixed-width guest addresses, explicit little-endian callbacks, executable hash checks, per-instruction state/budget boundaries, precise fault PCs, SVC unwinding to the existing scheduler, invalidation through writable aliases and visible bounded fallback. CROs and unsupported FP modes fail; complete ARM/VFP semantics are not claimed.
  • Actual external AOT execution of the original framebuffer NCCH matches the interpreter's registers/flags and every mapped memory region across uneven budgets. Two touched/button frames execute 1,382,534 instructions, four SVCs, zero fallback and orderly exit. Framebuffer files match byte-for-byte.
  • A separate original 30-instruction ARM/Thumb/scalar-VFP probe checks single and double arithmetic, flags, stack transfers, state switching and one-instruction resume. Its registers/VFP words match Unicorn 2.1.4 independently.
  • External integration passes 566 boundary/frame assertions +34 ABI probe +297 service-startup assertions +35 CP15 assertions +1,778 heap assertions +639 shared/sync +858 HID +3,603 direct HID/GSP display assertions, plus the v0.11 relay probe under GCC, ASan/UBSan, Clang and emulated 32-bit big-endian PowerPC Linux/QEMU 750. Emulation does not establish Wii U behavior or useful speed.
  • Real devkitPPC/WUT builds produce native, original-AOT guest and external-AOT guest RPX/WUHB artifacts. The new external build starts in guest mode and uses the existing TV/GamePad/input/exit platform implementation.
  • stage-title copies a validated RPX and generates original code/content/meta metadata and checker artwork. It refuses system IDs and existing destinations. Its launch settings are candidates needing console validation. It produces staging, not an installable WUP; no certificate/key/donor is included.
  • pack-title streams four encrypted 0x2001 FST/code/content/meta contents, original TMD/ticket rights and locally supplied certificates. It wraps a fresh title key with a user-local common key and publishes only after independent decryption/hash/FST/source-file checks. It refuses changed/unsafe inputs and existing outputs. Signatures are absent; FST/COS/installer behavior is pending. No credentials are bundled or printed. verify-package is a separate reader.
  • The real WUT RPX passes staging -> encrypted packing -> independent verification and both CLI paths with spaces/Japanese characters. Synthetic key/certificates only, temporary package deleted; this is not an installable title. OpenSSL independently decrypts the ticket and all four test contents. Wrong keys, repaired-hash malicious FSTs, corruption, mutation and cancellation are tested.
  • analyze-title writes a private exact-hash intake with segments, stack/BSS, section inventory, extended-header service allowlist and optional lifter analysis. Declared service access is not executed/implemented IPC. No intake enables a compatibility profile. Mario investigation leads and the proposed gameplay/save route are in docs/first-title.md.
  • Existing strict NCCH/NCSD/ExeFS/BLZ/SMDH inspection, safe loader, bounded CPU, single-guest events/handles/sleep/debug/exit, native diagnostics and structural WUP validator remain tested. Commercial build still fails without publishing a placeholder package. Paths with spaces/Japanese characters work on Linux.
  • 155 Python tests, 51,202 CPU assertions, 222 original-guest assertions, 1,001 service, 306 heap, 197 kernel, 336 HID, 508 direct GSP, 762 relay, 160 stack/transfer and 310 thread assertions pass; all C suites also pass ASan/UBSan. The complete v0.13.0 runner passes 36 checks, including all 14 external probes normally/sanitized, four RPX/WUHB builds, all five compiled libctru applications normally/sanitized, staging/intake, offline encrypted packaging and installed-wheel inspection/execution/packing. Required make check and make prototype pass. Crypto and ARM/libctru are optional external developer dependencies.

October 12 decryption-backend decision: the user's Batch CIA 3DS Decryptor request was investigated against pinned original/Redux/readable-engine sources. Its AES and seed methods fit the already implemented preparation stage. Retain the original local-key engine and validate decrypted CIA/CCI/CXI handoffs; do not add opaque Windows binaries, inherited-source license ambiguity, wildcard cleanup or network seed fetching to the GUI pipeline. A source-built external backend remains an option only if a concrete unmet requirement justifies it. See the audit and its original CCI handoff proof.

Maintained source: docs/development-history.md · 0b5e01cd0abc. GitHub source and issue links may require repository access.

External development tools¶

The optional compiler-built homebrew workflow links original applications with unmodified devkitPro/libctru at 9b55eda44cf80b971503991e0c78f9bc8fe50425, https://github.com/devkitPro/libctru, using its zlib-style permission in README.md. The complete notice is preserved at docs/licenses/libctru.txt. Library source and devkitARM remain external dependencies; their source/tool binaries are not included in the source bundle. The optional compiled interactive RPX/WUHB embeds that original application's ARM executable, including linked libctru and GCC/newlib support. Keep the libctru notice and existing runtime license texts with that artifact. The ARM compiler used in this workflow is GCC 15.2.0; the GCC exception/GPL texts are the same license versions preserved below, not a claim that it is GCC 16.1.0 or that the ARM and PPC newlib builds are identical. ARM newlib's complete collected notices are also preserved as docs/licenses/COPYING.NEWLIB.ARM (devkitPro/newlib devkitPro license-reference commit 57bd8e1ade3cf46d2e61a72516ac8bfaf8fdfd62). The linked 3DSX CRT is devkitPro/devkitarm-crtls under MPL 2.0. Its unmodified 3dsx_crt0.s, 3dsx.ld and 3dsx.specs source from commit 1c0c10257c44bbb5a433453bb6bba91582825492 is included at docs/licenses/devkitarm-crtls/, with the full MPL text at docs/licenses/MPL-2.0.txt. The rebuilt .crt0 section bytes and relocations match the installed ARM SDK object; whole-object hashes differ. These sources accompany the covered startup code in the optional compiled guest. They are not project-authored code or linked into the native PPC platform as assembly. See the audit receipt at docs/licenses/devkitarm-crtls/README.md. CTR2WUP's license does not replace their MPL.

The separate CTR2WUP-CIA-Intake Windows x64 utility bundles Microsoft's .NET 10.0.12 runtime and apphost under MIT plus the preserved third-party notices from the exact restored runtime/host packages. Their LICENSE.TXT and THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.TXT are identical for those packages and accompany the helper distribution as LICENSE-DOTNET.txt and THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES-DOTNET.txt. Copies are also at docs/licenses/dotnet-runtime-*.txt. This limited console helper is original intake code, not the final GUI/injector; no SDK, user game or credential is bundled. Runtime source: https://github.com/dotnet/runtime, v10.0.12.

Encrypted packaging optionally calls the external Python package cryptography 50.0.0, https://github.com/pyca/cryptography. Its Apache-2.0 OR BSD-3-Clause license permits the BSD-3-Clause grant selected here; the full notice is preserved at docs/licenses/cryptography-LICENSE.BSD. No cryptography, OpenSSL, CFFI or other native dependency binary is bundled in these source/diagnostic archives. A future Windows dependency distribution must retain all applicable package and native dependency notices.

Rust, Cargo, GCC/devkitPPC, newlib, elf2rpl and optional SDK packages remain external toolchain dependencies. This notice does not assert that their licenses are identical to WUT's. Redistributing a complete host application or toolchain bundle requires a dependency-by-dependency notice and source audit. No ROM, commercial game assets, Nintendo SDK, donor title or credential is included with this project.

The optional scene encoding tests use external, unmodified nihstro headers at f4d8659decbfe5d234f04134b5002b82dc515a44 (BSD-3-Clause, copyright 2014 Tony Wasserka). They are compiled only in a separate reference test, not copied, linked into the runtime or bundled in distributions. No emulator implementation is incorporated by the original loader/MOV shader/software scene increment.

Runtime notices for the diagnostic artifact¶

The diagnostic links WUT and GCC/newlib support. Runtime license texts accompany the source/artifact distribution in docs/licenses: - COPYING.NEWLIB: devkitPro/newlib devkitPPC branch, license-reference commit 729f71403f8f3e9941049d0d707def766553f5cb. This collects the individual BSD/MIT and other newlib contributor notices; it is not a claim of identical source revision to every SDK library object. - COPYING.RUNTIME and COPYING3: GCC 16.1.0 source commit 6afcc4f6da931eb93f3ab001a0dd9650ea71d1ea, GCC Runtime Library Exception 3.1 and its referenced GPLv3 text. The exception addresses eligible compiled target-code combinations; the external compiler itself is not bundled.

The full devkitPro SDK/image, external lifter binary and unlicensed packaging candidate binaries are not part of the delivered source/diagnostic bundles.

Maintained source: THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES · 0b5e01cd0abc. GitHub source and issue links may require repository access.

Launch music check (0.20.0)¶

Reviewed October 13: Audio Check's full trace records silence and the native tone PASS; GX Transfers has matching two-step PASS result/trace files. The user confirms launch music stopped and both apps exited correctly. The separate Audio Check result TXT was not included; its complete trace supplies the named observations. This focused regression is complete and needs no repeat. See the evidence review. The procedure below is retained for a future audio regression, not a pending test request.

The latest Save, RomFS, SD asset and GX tests passed their scoped checks. The follow-up Save trace confirms persistence across closing and reopening the app. Those checks do not need repeating. See the evidence review.

The Wii U launch sound continued throughout the apps. This build takes a fresh native AX audio context on launch and releases it on exit/foreground changes. Compiled guests do not acquire a tone voice or implement 3DS audio. Host checks and Wii U cross-builds verify the code; hearing silence needs this short round.

Run these two apps¶

  1. Copy the ZIP's sd-card/ contents to your SD root. Keep sd:/ctr2wup/ and all its reports and saves. Launch from Aroma as before; no WUP installation or ROM/key is needed.
  2. Open CTR2WUP Audio Check 0.20.0. Turn up TV and GamePad volume. At the launch-music prompt press R if it is quiet, or L if the launch sound still plays. At the next prompt press A for the short native tone, then R if it plays through both TV and GamePad, or L to record a problem. Press Minus to exit.
  3. Open CTR2WUP GX Transfers 0.20.0. At the two picture prompts confirm with R, or report an incorrect picture with L. The TV should show green, then red, then green; the GamePad stays blue. No test tone should play. Press Minus to exit.

If an app hangs or displays an audio/storage error, stop that run and return any files it saved. Repeating a failure is not needed to produce a report.

Send the results here¶

The apps display RESULT VERIFIED and the result filename. Plain-text files are in sd:/ctr2wup/:

  • result-native-audio-0.20.0-*.txt
  • result-compiled-gx-transfer-0.20.0-*.txt
  • Matching native-audio-*.txt and compiled-gx-transfer-*.txt full traces, if present.

Upload those new files in this chat, together or as a ZIP below 32 MiB. Add one short note stating whether launch music stopped in both apps and whether Minus returned normally in both. Pictures are optional. The completed Save/RomFS checks need no new upload.

Purpose and limits¶

Audio Check records launch-music silence and the native tone on both outputs. GX confirms that the same startup does not disrupt a compiled original guest or its transfer pictures. Reports record AX initialization, inherited-context resets and cleanup counters. An initialized AX context alone cannot prove audible silence. These tests do not establish 3DS guest audio, PICA draws, commercial gameplay or an installed WUP.

Maintained source: docs/launch-audio.md · 0b5e01cd0abc. GitHub source and issue links may require repository access.
